2026-10-18T09:20:42.0081922Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve the Firmus max bundle image again with the Decode cap back at 32 per rank so Decode MegaMoE stays on SwapAB

Run 39110496019 (823b79f, image a8ed4592 built by that run) scored
7082.82 against 7567.56 for 39094011392. Prefill improved as intended
(steady TTFT 1817 -> 1528 ms, TTFT failures 125 -> 85, Prefill MegaMoE
1828 -> 1695 us per call, Decode GPU idle removed: busy 83% -> 98.5%),
but Decode got slower: steady ITL 15.0 -> 17.9 ms, ITL failures 1 -> 137.

Cause, from the profile pass kernel summary: with the cap at 136 the
Decode ranks sat at 33-34 running requests, i.e. 132-136 verify tokens
per rank. DeepGEMM 0.1.5 selects SwapAB only for num_tokens <= 128
(csrc/jit_kernels/heuristics/sm90_mega_moe.hpp:98), so 43898 of 48880
Decode MegaMoE calls (90%) ran the non-SwapAB BLOCK_M 64 path at 229 us,
where none of the five Decode MegaMoE patches apply, and the remaining
SwapAB calls took 269 us waiting for the slower ranks in the shared EP
collective. The cap model behind 136 ignored this 128-token kernel
boundary.

This run changes only the runtime config on top of the image built from
823b79f (this branch is created from that commit, as the repository
rules ask for option changes on an existing build): Decode cap 128 (32
per DP rank, cuda-graph batch sizes up to 32) and images.sglang pinned to
39110496019's image. Everything else, including concurrency 192, the
four runtime switches and the kernel chain, is unchanged, so this is the
bundle's intended configuration with every Decode patch active.

Validation: pytest passes (the reference test now pins the cap at 128).
